### ROOT Subsystem Documentation

#### 1. Project Overview
The `ROOT` subsystem is a comprehensive suite designed for advanced seismic tomography and geophysical analysis, with a strong focus on Africa. It integrates various tools and modules to handle seismic wave analysis, data visualization, model generation, and high-performance computing. The system is structured into several key sub-modules:

- **6_ShareWithGroup**: Geophysical properties and tomographic data visualization using MATLAB scripts.
- **7_AFANT**: Seismic tomography tasks in HPC environments with specialized job scripts and configurations.
- **2_Data**: Detailed GeoJSON data for tectonic plates and other geophysical datasets.
- **X_Bin** and **X_Bin_copy**: Advanced crustal analysis and surface wave propagation modeling.
- **lovee_codes-master** and **raylee_codes-master**: Simulation and inversion of Love and Rayleigh waves, respectively.
- **5_Result**: Visualization of tomographic data.
- **4_Bin**: Integration of multiple languages for comprehensive seismic analysis.
